# F2 Code Quality Evidence - Lint / Format / Typecheck

## Command Results
- `bun run lint`: FAILED (exit 1)
- `bun run fmt:check`: FAILED (exit 1)
- `bun run typecheck`: FAILED (exit 2)

## Findings
- Lint did not reach source linting. `bunx oxlint --config oxlint.config.ts loom leyline conduit packages` failed because the installed oxlint wrapper reports it is IDE-extension-only and says to run `vp lint` instead.
- Format check did not reach source formatting validation. `bunx oxfmt --check .` failed because the installed oxfmt wrapper reports it is IDE-extension-only and says to run `vp fmt` instead.
- Typecheck failed broadly. Primary failure classes captured in raw output include missing root JSX compiler configuration for TSX files (`TS17004`/`TS6142`), duplicate Effect package warnings, missing Loom path modules such as `@/core/debug`, `@/core/relay/lcu-transport`, and `@/core/types/branded`, plus `leyline/tests/unit/env-config.test.ts` `loadConfig` redeclarations.
- Explicit `any` scan across current `loom/src`, `conduit/src`, and `packages` found no authored component/service matches in changed files. Matches are limited to generated/test-support files: changed `loom/src/routeTree.gen.ts` generated TanStack Router `as any` entries, and unchanged `loom/src/bun-test.d.ts` Bun test global declarations.

```text
error: script "lint" exited with code 1
```
